
Da_explore_dig_2_effect = {
	random_list = {
		38 = {
			set_province_flag = DA_HX_m2_ore	# DA_HX find mark
			modifier = { factor = 2 has_province_flag = DA_HX_run_prospectors }	# DA_HX Prospectors
			trigger = {
				NOT = { DA_prov_has_all_metals_common = yes }
			}
			province_event = { 
				id = DA_ADK_explore.17		# common metal
				days = 10
			}
		}
		40 = {
			set_province_flag = DA_HX_m2_lore	# DA_HX find mark

DA_Set_level_as_explored = {
	if = {
		limit = { 
			DA_has_dig_level_1_or_higher = yes
			NOT = { has_province_flag = DA_looked_dig_adk_2 }
		}
		custom_tooltip = DA_update_exploration_completion_2_3_tt
		set_province_flag = DA_looked_dig_adk_2
		hidden_effect = {
			add_permanent_province_modifier = {
				name = DA_local_explored_dig_2
				duration = -1
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { 
			DA_has_dig_level_2_or_higher = yes
			NOT = { has_province_flag = DA_looked_dig_adk_3 }
		}
		custom_tooltip = DA_update_exploration_completion_2_3_tt
		set_province_flag = DA_looked_dig_adk_3
		hidden_effect = {
			change_variable = {
				which = DA_exp_completion_rate_max
				value = 1
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_province_modifier = DA_local_explored_dig_2
			add_permanent_province_modifier = {
				name = DA_local_explored_dig_3
				duration = -1
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { 
			DA_has_dig_level_3_or_higher = yes
			NOT = { has_province_flag = DA_looked_dig_adk_4 }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			change_variable = {
				which = DA_exp_completion_rate
				value = 1
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = DA_update_exploration_completion_tt
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					which = DA_exp_completion_rate
					which = DA_exp_completion_rate_max
				}
			}
			set_province_flag = DA_looked_dig_adk_4
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_province_modifier = DA_local_explored_dig_3
				add_permanent_province_modifier = {
					name = DA_local_explored_dig_4
					duration = -1
				}
				set_variable = {
					which = DA_exp_completion_rate
					value = 0
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { 
			DA_has_dig_level_4_or_higher = yes
			NOT = { has_province_flag = DA_looked_dig_adk_5 }

					name = DA_local_explored_dig_11
					duration = -1
				}
				set_variable = {
					which = DA_exp_completion_rate
					value = 0
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else = { # should never go here
		add_base_tax = 1
		
	}
}
DA_Show_Exploration_completion_Rate = {
	$target$ = {
		custom_tooltip = DA_Hold_exploration_completion_tt
	}
}
DA_pre_setting_Dig_level_as_explored = {
	hidden_effect = {
		set_variable = {
			which = DA_exp_completion_rate_fake
			value = 0
		}
		change_variable = {
			which = DA_exp_completion_rate_fake
			which = DA_exp_completion_rate
		}
		change_variable = {
			which = DA_exp_completion_rate_fake
			value = 1
		}
	}
}
